Jake Powers
def combs(a):
    if len(a) == 0:
        return [[]]
    cs = []
    for c in combs(a[1:]):
        cs += [c, c+[a[0]]]
    return cs

>>> combs([1,2,3,4,5])
[[], [1], [2], [2, 1], [3], [3, 1], [3, 2], ..., [5, 4, 3, 2, 1]]

Jul 30, 2024  3:18:40 PM (Read by them after 1 second)
Me
Semi-complex SQL question (AWS Glue Visual ETL specifically): how would you write a transform for an incorrectly imputed mixed case name (ex. LaSHAE) and convert it to the correct Pascal Case (ex. LaShae)???

Me and ChatGPT have been struggling with the syntax for two hours now. And yes, Regex is allowed, but Glue has some constraints on it. I would also rather use Regex but it’s being finicky.

Here is the current SQL (everything is functioning apart from Pascal Case handling):

CASE
    WHEN LENGTH(TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), ' ', -1))) = 1 
        AND LENGTH(TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), ' ', 1))) > 1
    THEN TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), ' ', 1))
    ELSE 
        CASE
            -- Adjustment for double-barrelled (hyphenated) names
            WHEN INSTR(TRIM(`First Name`), '-') > 0 THEN
                CONCAT(
                    INITCAP(LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), '-', 1)))), '-',
                    INITCAP(LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), '-', -1))))
                )
            -- Adjustment for names with apostrophes
            WHEN INSTR(TRIM(`First Name`), "'") > 0 THEN
                CONCAT(
                    INITCAP(LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), "'", 1)))), "'",
                    INITCAP(LOWER(SUBSTRING_INDEX(TRIM(`First Name`), "'", -1)))
                )
            -- Adjustment for names with mixed case
            WHEN TRIM(`First Name`) != INITCAP(TRIM(LOWER(`First Name`))) THEN
                CONCAT(
                    UPPER(SUBSTRING(TRIM(`First Name`), 1, 1)),
                    LOWER(
                        CASE
                            WHEN LENGTH(TRIM(`First Name`)) > 1 THEN 
                                SUBSTRING(TRIM(`First Name`), 2, LENGTH(TRIM(`First Name`)) - 1)
                            ELSE ''
                        END
                    )
                )
            ELSE 
                INITCAP(TRIM(`First Name`))
        END
END AS adjusted_first_name,

And this is the print function:
void printResultLine(const DirectoryEntry *entry, const char *status, int fileIndex) {
    (void)fileIndex; // suppresses -wextra warning

    char displayName[13];

    if (entry->isDeleted) {
        displayName[0] = '_';
        strncpy(displayName + 1, entry->fileName + 1, 7);
        displayName[8] = '\0';
    } else {
        strncpy(displayName, entry->fileName, 8);
        displayName[8] = '\0';
    }

    char fullName[256];
    if (entry->path[0]) {
        snprintf(fullName, sizeof(fullName), "/%s/%s%s%s",
                 entry->path,
                 displayName,
                 entry->extension[0] ? "." : "",
                 entry->extension);
    } else {
        snprintf(fullName, sizeof(fullName), "/%s%s%s",
                 displayName,
                 entry->extension[0] ? "." : "",
                 entry->extension);
    }

    // Determine if filename is "random"
    const char *image = getenv("IMG"); // or pass image name into context
    int useTabs = 1;

    if (image && strstr(image, "random"))
        useTabs = 0;

    if (useTabs) {
        // Tab-separated, matching simple/simple2/evenlesssimple
        printf("FILE\t%s\t%s\t%u\n", status, fullName, entry->fileSize);
    } else {
        // Space-separated random format
        printf("FILE    %-6s  %s  %u\n", status, fullName, entry->fileSize);
    }
}

What on earth am I doing wrong? (Other than the spacing between file and status for random).
